    Outlook for FY2023/2024                                                 二零二三╱二零二四財政年度展望
    We have confidence in the long-term profitable growth trajectory of     我們對業務的長期盈利增長軌道充滿信心。
    our business.

    In Mainland China, we will continue to focus on improving the           於中國內地，我們將繼續專注以嶄新市場推廣活
    execution and expansion of our core VITASOY and VITA brand              動，提升我們的核心維他奶及維他品牌組合的銷
    portfolio with new marketing campaigns, while selectively identifying   售執行及擴展，同時揀選及推動具長期潛力的策
    and driving strategic innovation that has long term potential.          略創新產品。

    In the Hong Kong SAR and the Macau SAR, we will sustain the growth      於香港特別行政區及澳門特別行政區，我們產品
    of our core products while promoting product innovation, which          組合的人均消耗較高，團隊將繼續推動創新產品
    we believe is feasible given the high per capita consumption of our     並同時保持核心產品增長。
    portfolio products.

    Our Australia and New Zealand business will fortify its leadership      我們的澳洲及新西蘭業務將於植物奶領域上穩固
    position in plant-based milk and further develop the new plant-based    領導地位，並進一步開發創新植物性乳酪產品平
    yoghurt platform.                                                       台。

    In ASEAN, we will improve the performance of our tofu category and      於東南亞，我們將提升豆腐產品的表現，並推出
    scale up beverage sales in both Singapore and the Philippines, along    新系列植物奶以加速於新加坡及菲律賓的飲品銷
    with our newly launched plant milks.                                    售。

    As a responsible company, we will also enhance our Environmental,       作為一家負責任的公司，我們將繼續提高環境、
    Social and Governance (ESG) performance and continue to                 社會及管治績效，並於達成範疇一、二及三溫室

    scope 1, 2 and 3 greenhouse gas emission inventory.

A note of appreciation                                                  致謝
I would like to express my gratitude to our Board of Directors,         本人謹藉此機會，衷心感謝我們的董事會、管理
management team, and staff for their diligent work and unwavering       層團隊及全體員工辛勤工作，以及對我們業務及
faith in our business and long-term growth potential. I would also      長遠增長潛力所抱有的堅定信念。本人亦謹此感
like to thank our shareholders, customers, suppliers and business       謝我們的股東、客戶、供應商及業務合作夥伴對
partners for their continued confidence in us.                          我們的持續信任。

Based on our portfolio of popular brands, product knowledge             基於我們備受歡迎的品牌組合、產品知識及組織
and organisational capabilities, I remain confident in our ability to   能力，本人對我們能長期錄得溢利以及業務增長
generate profits and growth over the long term.                         的能力充滿信心。




Winston Yau-lai LO                                                      羅友禮

Group revenue increased 2% net of currency impact, while falling           撇除匯率影響後，本集團收入增長 2%，但由於
slightly by 2% in Hong Kong dollar terms due to Renminbi and               人民幣及澳元貶值，以港幣計算的收入則輕微下
Australian dollar depreciation.                                            降 2%。

During the year, we successfully recorded a profit from operations         年內，儘管原材料價格及公用開支成本高企，我
of HK$104 million versus a loss of HK$213 million last year, despite       們仍錄得經營溢利港幣 104,000,000 元，而去年
increased raw material prices and utility costs. This result was mainly    則錄得虧損港幣 213,000,000 元，主要由於有效
attributable to effective cost rationalisation across our operations       成本控制及中國內地業務的推廣，以及與去年相
and promotions in Mainland China, as well as higher government             比香港業務獲得的政府補貼增加所致。我們亦採
subsidies received by our Hong Kong Operation compared with last           取特選定價措施，以緩解上漲的原材料價格壓
year. We also took selective pricing action to alleviate the increase in   力。
higher raw material prices.

Profit attributable to equity shareholders of the Company was              本公司股權持有人應佔溢利為港幣 46,000,000
HK$46 million, compared with a loss of HK$159 million last year.           元，而去年為虧損港幣 159,000,000 元。業績與
The result was in line with the profit alert announcement issued on        二零二三年五月十九日公佈的盈利預告公告一
19th May 2023.                                                             致。

We remain confident about the long-term potential of the plant-            我們對以植物為本市場的長期潛力及增長能力充

disciplined focus on our core fundamentals and expand product              務，擴大產品銷售渠道，以提高收入及溢利。
availability to improve revenue and profit.




                                                                                     Annual Report 2022/2023 年度報告   11
              Group Chief Executive Officer’s Report/Business Review
              集 團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧




     Environmental, Social and Governance (ESG)                                環境、社會及管治
     During the year, we updated our list of ESG priorities through an         年內，我們根據雙重重要性評估的流程，透過廣
     extensive stakeholder engagement exercise, which was conducted            泛持份者的參與，更新了我們環境、社會及管治
     according to the double materiality assessment process.                   議題上的優先重要性。

     We also made progress towards meeting the targets established in          我們在實現維他奶可持續發展框架中設定的目標
     our Vitasoy Sustainability Framework. This framework consists of          方面亦取得進展。該框架以「生產適當的產品」
     two pillars: “Making the Right Products” and “Making Products the         及「採用適當的生產方法」兩大支柱為基礎。
     Right Way”.

     building a product portfolio commensurate with developing plant-          高營養價值、較低糖及飽和脂肪的產品組合，並

     the front of our product packaging. “Making Products the Right Way”       過整個集團減排藍圖、減少用水及能源消耗和廢
     is our commitment to reducing our environmental impact through            棄物零堆填。我們亦確保我們的工作場所安全及
     a Group-wide emissions reduction roadmap, as well as reducing             具包容性。參與營運地區有關社會環境等項目，
     water and energy consumption and eliminating manufacturing waste          並鼓勵供應商提高其環境、社會及管治績效。
     to landfill. We also ensured our workplaces are safe and inclusive,

     Tax Strategy                                                               稅務策略
     When considering tax, the Group gives due consideration to the             當考慮稅務時，本集團會適當考慮其企業及社會
     importance of its corporate and social responsibilities. More              責任的重要性。更明確而言，本集團堅持於其創
     specifically, the Group commits to paying taxes in the countries           造價值的國家中繳納稅項，並確保其全面遵守所
     where it creates value and ensuring it is fully compliant with tax laws    有相關司法管轄區的稅務法例。本集團同時堅持
     across all relevant jurisdictions. The Group also commits to following     遵循經濟合作與發展組織轉讓定價指引，並確保
     the Organisation for Economic Co-operation and Development                 集團公司間的交易時常遵從公平原則。此外，本
     (OECD) transfer pricing guidelines and to ensuring that the arm’s          集團就集團的稅項事務對稅務機關一直保持公開
     length principle is always observed in transactions between Group          透明，並且披露相關資料讓稅務機關能執行其覆

     transparent with tax authorities about the Group’s tax affairs and to

     Financial Risk Management                                                  財務風險管理

     treasury management system for all subsidiaries. Borrowings are            營運附屬公司一般以當地貨幣進行借貸，為當地
     normally taken out in local currencies by the operating subsidiaries       投資項目提供資金及減低部份外匯風險。
     to fund their investments and partially mitigate foreign currency
     risks.

     Board Independence                                                       董事會獨立性
     The Company recognises that Board independence is pivotal in good        本公司深知董事會獨立性對良好企業管治及董事
     corporate governance and board effectiveness. The Board believes         會效能至關重要。董事會相信，本公司已設立有
     that the Company has effective mechanisms in place to ensure             效機制可確保本公司任何董事的獨立觀點及意見
     independent views and input from any director of the Company             能夠傳達予董事會，以增加決策的客觀性和成效
     are conveyed to the Board for enhancing an objective and effective       性。董事會通過其薪酬及提名委員會（「薪酬及
     decision making. The governance framework and mechanisms are             提名委員會」）定期審查管治框架及機制，以確
     under regular review by the Board, through its Remuneration and          保其成效。薪酬及提名委員會於二零二三年六月
     Nomination Committee (“the R&N Committee”), to ensure their              就該等機制進行以下檢討：
     effectiveness. The R&N Committee had performed the review of
     such mechanisms in June 2023 as follows:

                                                公司以有效履行其職責及責任。董事於二零二二╱二零二三財政年
                                                度的會議出席記錄亦於企業管治報告中披露。

Long Serving INEDs (with tenure for more than   In respect of the re-election of an INED who has served on the
nine years)                                     Board for more than nine years, the Board and the R&N Committee
連任多年的獨立非執行董事（任期超過九年）                            would take account of additional factors and reasons to justify why
                                                they consider the long serving director is still independent so as
                                                to ensure independent views and comments are sought from long
                                                serving INED.
                                                就重選任職董事會逾九年的獨立非執行董事而言，董事會與薪酬及
                                                提名委員會將考慮不同因素及理由確定任職多年的董事仍然獨立，
                                                從而確保可連任多年的獨立非執行董事的觀點及意見均為獨立。

Director Nomination Policy                      The Policy sets out the selection and nomination criteria and
董事提名政策                                          procedures for appointment/re-appointment of Directors.
                                                Independence is one of the factors to be considered for
                                                appointment/re-appointment of Directors, in particular for INED
                                                appointment.
                                                該政策載有委任╱重新委任董事的甄選及提名標準以及程序。獨立
                                                性是考慮委任╱重新委任董事（尤其是獨立非執行董事）的因素之
                                                一。

                                                The R&N Committee would take account of the independence
                                                factor in accordance with the Policy before making
                                                recommendations to the Board for approval of the appointment/re-
                                                appointment of an INED.

                                                事的建議前，根據該政策考慮獨立性因素。

     Key Features or Mechanisms                        Implementation and Review of Effectiveness
     主要特點或機制                                           執行及檢討成效

     Directors Conflict of Interest Policy             The Company has policy and procedures in place to avoid any
     董事利益衝突政策                                          potential conflict of interests and not to weaken the objectivity and
                                                       integrity of the Board for decisions making. Under the Policy, any
                                                       Director has to declare his/her directorships outside the Company,
                                                       interests in controlling company, public office appointment and
                                                       any other interests that might be considered prejudicial to his/
                                                       her independence and has to declare his/her interests in any
                                                       transaction in which he/she may have interest and abstain from
                                                       voting on such transaction.
                                                       本公司已制定政策及程序以避免任何潛在利益衝突，且不會削弱董
                                                       事會決策的客觀性及誠信性。根據該政策，任何一位董事必須申報
                                                       其於本公司以外的董事職務、於控股公司中的權益、公職任命及任
                                                       何其他可能被認為有礙其獨立性的利益，並須申報其於可能擁有利
                                                       益的任何交易中的權益，並就該交易放棄投票。

     Open Board Culture                                INEDs in family businesses will generally play a more active role in
     開明的董事會文化                                          corporate governance area. Our Board culture under the leadership
                                                       of Executive Chairman, encourages questions and challenges from
                                                       directors in particular from INEDs who may hold different views
                                                       from the Executive Directors and Senior Management and their
                                                       comments and concerns will be closely followed up and addressed
                                                       by the Executive Directors and Senior Management.
                                                       家族企業中的獨立非執行董事一般會於企業管治方面發揮更為積極
                                                       的作用。在執行主席領導下，我們的董事會文化鼓勵董事（尤其是
                                                       獨立非執行董事）提出問題及質疑，彼等可能與執行董事及高層管
                                                       理人員持有不同觀點，而執行董事及高層管理人員將密切跟進及應
                                                       對彼等的意見及關注。

     No equity-based remuneration with performance-    Director fees payable to Director, including INEDs, are fixed fees
     related elements to INEDs                         without any discretionary element nor they are remunerated with
     獨立非執行董事未曾授予與表現績效相關的股本                             equity-based awards. Directors Fees are also determined by the
     權益酬金                                              R&N Committee and the Board with reference to the roles and
                                                       responsibilities taken up by the Director and benchmarking against
                                                       peers.
                                                       應付董事（包括獨立非執行董事）的董事袍金為固定數額，並無任
                                                       何酌情因素，而酬金亦無包括以股權為基礎的獎勵。董事袍金亦由
                                                       薪酬及提名委員會與董事會參照董事所承擔的角色及職責以及行業
                                                       標準而釐定。

Board Proceedings                                                          董事會程序
The Board met five times in FY2022/2023 to discuss and approve the         董事會於二零二二╱二零二三財政年度內召開五
Group’s long term strategic plan; to review and monitor the financial      次會議，討論及批准本集團之長期策略規劃；審
and business performance of the Group and each of its operating            視及監察本集團及各營運單位之財務及業務表
units; to consider and approve annual budget, the financial reports;       現；考慮及審批年度預算及財務報告；審閱及批
to review and approve sustainability report and various corporate          准可持續發展報告及與企業管治及可持續發展相
governance and sustainability related policies and practices; and to       關之各種政策及常規；並監察及審視本集團風險
monitor and review the effectiveness of the Group’s risk management        管理表現之成效。
performance.

The Board and its Committees are supplied with full and timely             董事會及其委員會均適時獲得本公司所有相關資
information in relation to the Company, which enables them to              料，以履行職責。本公司已有既定程序致使董事
discharge their responsibilities. There are established procedures         在適當的情況下，為履行其職責及責任而諮詢獨
for Directors to seek independent professional advice for them to          立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
discharge their duties and responsibilities, where appropriate, at the
Company’s expenses.

     Major responsibilities         •    to make recommendations to the Board on the appointment, re-appointment and

                                         與外聘核數師開會討論審核工作之性質及範圍；

                                         are submitted to the Board;
                                         在提交予董事會前，審閱中期財務報告及全年財務報表；

                                    •    to discuss problems and reservations arising from the interim review and final audit,
                                         and any other matters the External Auditors may wish to discuss, and review the
                                         External Auditors’ management letter and management’s response;
                                         討論源於中期審閱及年結審核過程所發現之問題及得出之保留意見，及任何其他
                                         外聘核數師欲討論之事宜，以及審閱外聘核數師之審核情況說明函件及管理層之
                                         回應；

                                    •    to review the internal audit programs and to ensure co-ordination between the
                                         Internal and External Auditors, assess the effectiveness of the Company’s risk
                                         management and internal control systems which cover all material financial,
                                         operational and compliance controls, and ensure that the internal audit function is
                                         adequately resourced and has appropriate standing within the Group;
                                         審閱內部審核計劃並確保內部審計師及外聘核數師間之協調，評估本公司涵蓋所
                                         有重大財務、營運及合規監控方面的風險管理及內部監控系統之成效，以及確保
                                         內部審計職能獲足夠資源之支援及在本集團內保持適當之地位；

                                         in financial reporting, internal control or other matters; and to ensure proper

                                         檢討有關本公司僱員可在保密情況下對財務報告、內部監控或其他事項之可能不

     The Board has approved a Whistleblowing Policy in March 2012             於二零一二年三月，董事會已批准採納舉報政
     which has been revised in March 2022. The main objective of the          策，並於二零二二年三月作出修訂。政策之主要
     Policy is to provide employees and external parties an open and          目標是為僱員及外間人士提供一個公開及統一之
     unified reporting channel and procedures to report any serious           舉報渠道及程序，甚至接納匿名舉報任何涉及本
     misconduct or malpractice involving the Company and its employees        公司及其僱員之嚴重失當或不良行為。審核委員
     even on an anonymous basis. The Audit Committee has been                 會獲委派全權負責監察及檢討舉報政策之實施及
     delegated with the overall responsibility for monitoring and reviewing   成效。
     the implementation and effectiveness of the Whistleblowing Policy.

     The Company has adopted a Group Business Ethics Policy in March          於二零二零年三月，本公司已採納集團商業道德
     2020 to establish the general principles of business ethical standards   政策，以制定本公司所有僱員於日常營運時應遵
     that all employees of the Company are expected to follow in daily        守之商業道德標準的一般原則。每名僱員必須承
     operation. Every employee is required to undertake to adhere             諾遵守該政策，包括處理利益衝突、保護及適當
     to this policy which includes provisions dealing with conflict of        使用本公司財產、賄賂及貪污、不誠實及欺詐行
     interests, protection and proper use of company assets, bribery          為等條文，以及舉報潛在違法及不當行為之程
     and corruption, dishonesty and fraudulent act as well as reporting       序。本公司對於違反該政策採取零容忍態度，絕
     procedures for potential illegality and misconduct. The Company          不容忍任何違法及不道德行為。審核委員會全面
     applies zero tolerance to any violation of this policy and shall not     負責檢討反貪污之內部監控系統。
     tolerate any illegal or unethical acts. The Audit Committee has the
     overall responsibility for reviewing the internal control system of
     anti-corruption.

     implementation and effectiveness of the Whistleblowing Policy and

     and markets. On appointment, new Directors will also be given an           責任及職責。就任須知亦附有董事會程序（包括

to Board approval. The Company Secretary reports to both the              書向執行主席及集團行政總裁匯報，並就有關董

Using risk assessment methodology and taking into account the            集團內部審計編製由審核委員會審批的年度審計

of operation, and operating environment, Group Internal Audit            管理層的關注範疇、營運性質及複雜性以及經營

the Audit Committee. The risk assessment methodology assists             計之頻率。集團內部審計按照已批核的審計計劃
in identifying business risks and determining audit frequencies.         安排內部審核程序，就財務、業務及職能方面之
Based on the approved audit plan, Group Internal Audit schedules         各項營運及活動進行獨立檢討，並安排將其資源
its internal audit programs to conduct an independent review of          集中處理高風險的範疇。
different financial, business and functional operations and activities
with resources focusing on areas with higher risk.

     Ethics Policy and Whistleblowing System                                    道德政策及舉報制度

     Risk Governance Structure                                                風險管治架構
     The risk governance structure consists of three layers of roles and      風險管治架構包括下列三個層面之角色及職責：
     responsibilities as explained below:


      Layer                                                Roles and Responsibilities
      層面                                                   職務及職責

      Oversight                                            Audit Committee, acting on behalf of the Board
      監督                                                   •    Oversees the Company’s risk management and internal
                                                                control on an ongoing basis.
                                                           •    Oversees those risks that warrant the Committee’s attention
                                                                and supervise risk management process as part of good
                                                                corporate governance.
                                                           代表董事會之審核委員會
                                                           •    持續監督本公司之風險管理及內部監控。
                                                           •    監督委員會需要注意之該等風險並監督風險管理程序，以配
                                                                合良好企業管治。

                                                           Group Internal Audit
                                                           •   Conducts independent review on the effectiveness of risk
                                                               management and internal control system and provides
                                                               reasonable assurance to Audit Committee.
                                                           集團內部審計
                                                           •   對風險管理及內部監控系統之成效進行獨立審閱，並向審核
                                                               委員會提供合理保證。

      Risk Monitoring & Review                             Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee & Entity CEOs
      風險監察及審閱                                              •    Design, implement and monitor the risk management and
                                                                internal control system.
                                                           •    Provide assurance to the Audit Committee on the
                                                                effectiveness of risk management and internal control system.
                                                           企業風險管理執行委員會及地方公司行政總裁
                                                           •    設計、實施及監察風險管理及內部監控系統。
                                                           •    向審核委員會提供有關風險管理及內部監控系統成效之保證。

                                                           Group Risk Management, an independent team under Group
                                                           Internal Audit and Risk Management
                                                           •     Supports the Board of Directors, Audit Committee and
                                                                 Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee.
                                                           •     Develops and implements risk management frameworks,
                                                                 policies, tools and methodologies with all subsidiaries.
                                                           •     Monitors status of risk and communicate with the Executive
                                                                 Committee and Audit Committee on an ongoing basis.
                                                           集團風險管理為集團內部審計及風險管理部門轄下之獨立團隊
                                                           •     支援董事會、審核委員會及企業風險管理執行委員會。
                                                           •     與所有附屬公司建立及執行風險管理框架、政策、工具及方
                                                                 法。
                                                           •     持續監察風險狀況，並與執行委員會及審核委員會持續溝通。

      Risk & Control Ownership                             Department Heads & Individual Employee
      風險及控制的責任                                             •   Ownership of managing risks, executing control activities and
                                                               identifying new risks in daily operation.
                                                           部門主管及個別員工
                                                           •   於日常營運中管理風險，執行監控活動及識別新風險。

Risk Management Process                                                  風險管理程序
Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,         我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財務
financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business        活動、合規、策略發展與業務規劃、投資決定及
planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes        外界因素，程序如下：
are as follows:

•    Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to         •   預測：所有員工均參與識別不同業務流程
     different business processes and relevant to the Company’s              及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威脅。
     objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual         為便於進行適當評估，我們將風險分為六
     staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including reputation,       個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、流動資
     strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation) to facilitate        金、信貸及營運）。
     the assessment appropriately.

•    Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of impact      •   評估：按照董事會設定之風險偏好，評估
     and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set by the             每項經識別風險的影響及可能性。風險之
     Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated according           影 響 及 可 能 性 將 根 據 過 往 記 錄、 統 計 分
     to the historical records, statistical analyses and relevant            析、相關經驗及管理層之專業判斷進行評
     experiences, specialist and expert judgments by management.             級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，包括協
     Group Risk Management facilitates the evaluation process                助企業風險管理執行委員會及審核委員會
     including the summary discussion in the Enterprise Risk                 之討論。
     Management Executive Committee and Audit Committee.

•    Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk            •   控制：各項風險將配對專責管理人員，成
     as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management            為風險負責人，以有效管理風險。風險負
     strategies and corresponding control owners are identified by           責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監控負責
     risk owner to deploy appropriate control activities.                    人，實施適當監控活動。

•    Regulate: We take a close monitoring on the current status of       •   監察：我們密切監控風險現況。各風險現
     risk, which is being monitored through key risk indicators with         況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設量度
     defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents, control            級別基準、所發生之相關事故、內部監控
     effectiveness and audit results. Any change in current status           程序之成效以及審計結果。風險現況倘出
     of risk to a worsened level will trigger a react process through        現任何不良轉變，均會啟動反應程序，展
     creation of a mitigation plan.                                          開減輕風險計劃。

•    React: When there is a change on the current status of risk,        •   緩解：當風險現況有所改變，相應負責人
     the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation plan with            將協調減輕風險計劃之行動細節、負責人
     action details, responsible person and timeline to address the          及時間表，以處理風險情況。
     status.

•    Report: To support the oversight of the Board on the                •   通報：風險報告內容及風險管理框架，均
     effectiveness of Vitasoy’s risk management, risk reporting              獲董事會批准，協助董事會監察維他奶風
     requirement was approved by the Board of Directors under                險管理之成效。集團風險管理定期向企業
     risk management framework. Periodic risk report is provided             風險管理執行委員會提供風險報告，監控
     to the Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee                   減輕風險計劃及現時風險管理實施之進
     summarizing the results of risk monitoring, mitigation                  度。此外，自推出管治、風險管理及合規
     plans and the current risk management implementation                    系統（「GRC 系統」）以來，企業風險管理
     progress. Besides, since the launch of the Governance, Risk             執行委員會及風險負責人可透過相關系統
     and Compliance System (“GRC System”), Enterprise Risk                   指標獲得實時風險狀況。
     Management Executive Committee and risk owners are able
     to obtain real-time risk status through the relevant system
     dashboards.

and dissemination of inside information, the Company is aware of        面，本公司知悉，根據上市規則及《證券及期貨
the requirements under the applicable Listing Rules and Securities      條例》之適用規定以及首要原則，本公司在得悉
and Futures Ordinance and the overriding principle that inside          內幕消息後，應於切實可行情況下盡快公佈有關
information should be announced as soon as reasonably practicable       資料，除非有關資料屬於《證券及期貨條例》中
after such information comes to our attention unless it falls within    任何安全港條文所界定者則另當別論。為遵守監
any of the safe harbours provisions under the Securities and Futures    管體系下有關內幕消息披露規定，本公司已有一
Ordinance. The Company has had a system in place with established       套擁有既定政策及程序之系統。董事會已於二零
policies and procedures for complying with the Inside Information       一二年十一月二十七日採納披露合規政策，並於
disclosure requirements under the regulatory regime. The Board          二零二一年十一月二十五日進一步作出一些細微
has adopted a Disclosure Compliance Policy on 27th November             修訂。該政策乃參照證券及期貨事務監察委員會
2012 which has been further revised with a few housekeeping             所頒佈之《內幕消息披露指引》而編製，旨在為
amendments on 25th November 2021. The Policy has been                   董事及僱員提供有關評估、匯報及發佈內幕消
formulated by reference to the “Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside      息、保密及遵守股份交易限制之指引。本公司已
Information” issued by the Securities and Futures Commission.           於行為守則及員工手冊內訂明嚴禁未經授權披露
The purpose of the Policy is to provide Directors and employees         或使用公司之機密資料以及內幕消息。
with guidelines on assessing, reporting and disseminating inside
information, maintaining confidentiality and abiding shares dealing
restrictions. The Company has included in its code of conduct and

use of confidential and inside information.

     Dividend Policy                                                            股息政策
     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to             董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股東
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution of            向董事會及管理層提供指引。
     surplus funds to its shareholders.

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following criteria       就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及考
     and consideration must be taken:                                           慮因素：

     –      Sharing of success with shareholders                                –   與股東共享成果
     –      Liquidity and capital requirement                                   –   流動資金及資本要求
     –      Historical trend of dividend payout                                 –   過往股息派付之趨勢
     –      Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                   –   與業內競爭對手之標準比較
     –      Fulfilment of financial covenants                                   –   符合財務契約
     –      Taxation impact                                                     –   稅務影響
     –      Restrictions                                                        –   限制條件
     –      Other factors that may deem relevant                                –   任何其他視為相關之因素

     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders to align          本公司計劃配合扣除一次性事件影響後之股東應
     with the fluctuation in profit attributable to shareholders net of one-    佔溢利之波動向股東宣派股息，並可能除中期或
     off event impact and may declare special dividends from time to time       末期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將不時酌
     in addition to interim or final dividends. The determination to pay        情決定是否派發股息以及所派發股息之水平。
     dividends and level of dividend to be distributed will be subject to the
     discretion of the Board from time to time.

Opinion                                                                   意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Vitasoy          本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第
International Holdings Limited (“the Company”) and its subsidiaries       116 至 250 頁的維他奶國際集團有限公司（以下
(“the Group”) set out on pages 116 to 250, which comprise the             簡稱「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集
consolidated statement of financial position as at 31st March             團」）的綜合財務報表，此財務報表包括於二零
2023, the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the consolidated      二三年三月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該
statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the           日止年度的綜合損益表、綜合損益及其他全面收
consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated          益表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及
cash flow statement for the year then ended and notes to the              綜合財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。
consolidated financial statements, including a summary of significant
accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and     我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at       公會頒布的《香港財務報告準則》真實而中肯地
31st March 2023 and of its consolidated financial performance and         反映了 貴集團於二零二三年三月三十一日的綜
its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance         合財務狀況及 貴集團截至該日止年度的綜合財
with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by         務表現及綜合現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條
the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”)        例》妥為擬備。
and have been properly prepared in compliance with the Hong Kong

We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on          我們已根據香港會計師公會頒布的《香港審計準

those standards are further described in the Auditor’s responsibilities   在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責
for the audit of the consolidated financial statements section of our     任」部分中作進一步闡述。根據香港會計師公
report. We are independent of the Group in accordance with the            會頒布的《專業會計師道德守則》（以下簡稱「守
HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (“the Code”) and     則」），我們獨立於 貴集團，並已履行守則中的
we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with   其他專業道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的審
the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is          計憑證能充足及適當地為我們的審計意見提供基

 1      Significant accounting policies                                  1     主要會計政策

 (a)    Statement of compliance                                          (a)   遵例聲明
 These financial statements have been prepared in accordance with         本財務報表是按照所有適用之《香港財務報告準
 all applicable Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”),       則》、香港公認會計原則及香港《公司條例》之規
 which collective term includes all applicable individual Hong Kong       定而編製。《香港財務報告準則》一詞包括香港
 Financial Reporting Standards, Hong Kong Accounting Standards            會計師公會頒佈之所有適用之個別《香港財務報
 (“HKASs”) and Interpretations issued by the Hong Kong Institute          告準則》、《香港會計準則》及詮釋。本財務報表
 of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting principles        亦符合香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則
 generally accepted in Hong Kong and the requirements of the Hong        （「上市規則」）之適用披露規定。本集團採納之
 Kong Companies Ordinance. These financial statements also comply         主要會計政策於下文披露。
 with the applicable disclosure provisions of the Rules Governing the
 Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited
 (the “Listing rules”). Significant accounting policies adopted by the
 Group are disclosed below.

 The HKICPA has issued certain amendments to HKFRSs that                 香港會計師公會已頒佈若干《香港財務報告準則》
 are first effective or available for early adoption for the current     之修訂本，並於本集團及本公司之當前會計期間
 accounting period of the Group and the Company. Note 1(c) provides      首次生效或可供提早採納。首次應用此等適用於
 information on any changes in accounting policies resulting from        本集團之新訂準則所引致於當前及過往會計期間
 initial application of these developments to the extent that they are   之任何會計政策變動已於本財務報表內反映，有
 relevant to the Group for the current and prior accounting periods      關資料載於附註 1(c)。
 reflected in these financial statements.

 Subsidiaries (including structured entities) are entities controlled by       附屬公司（包括結構性實體）為本集團所控制之
 the Group. The Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has            實體。當本集團從參與某實體之業務獲得或有權

 the ability to affect those returns through its power over the entity.        影響該等回報，則本集團控制該實體。當評估本
 When assessing whether the Group has power, only substantive                  集團是否有權力時，只考慮具體權利（由本集團
 rights (held by the Group and other parties) are considered.                  及其他人士持有）。

 A structured entity is an entity that has been designed so that voting        結構性實體則指那些在釐定誰是其控制人時、投
 or similar rights are not the dominant factor in deciding who controls        票權或類似權利並非重要考量的實體，例如當任
 the entity, such as when any voting rights relate to administrative           何投票權僅與行政工作有關，而且相關的業務活
 tasks only and the relevant activities are directed by means of               動是透過合約安排方式指導。
 contractual arrangements.

               the amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquiree and              控股權益金額與本集團先前持有被收購方
               the fair value of the Group’s previously held equity interest in            股本權益公允值之總和；超出
               the acquiree; over

       (ii)    the net fair value of the acquiree’s identifiable assets and         (ii)   於收購日期計量之被收購方可識別資產及
               liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                            負債之公允值淨額之部份。

 The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses          本集團為按攤銷成本計量之金融資產（包括現金
 (“ECLs”) on financial assets measured at amortised cost (including        及現金等值項目、應收賬款及其他應收款以及向

 Measurement of ECLs                                                       預期信貸虧損之計量
 ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses. Credit         預期信貸虧損乃信貸虧損之概率加權估計。信貸
 losses are measured as the present value of all expected cash             虧損以所有預期現金差額（即根據合約應付予本

 Group in accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the         間的差額）的現值計量。
 Group expects to receive).

 Where the effect of discounting is material, the expected cash            倘折現之影響屬重大，則預期現金差額將採用應
 shortfalls are discounted using the effective interest rate determined    收賬款及其他應收款初始確認時釐定之實際利率
 at initial recognition or an approximation thereof for trade and other    或其近似值折現。
 receivables.

 The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs is the                 估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮之最長期間為本集團
 maximum contractual period over which the Group is exposed to             面臨信貸風險之最長合約期間。
 credit risk.

 In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account reasonable and            在計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團考慮合理及有理
 supportable information that is available without undue cost or effort.   據而無需付出過多的成本或努力獲得之資料。這
 This includes information about past events, current conditions and       包括有關過往事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預
 forecasts of future economic conditions.                                  測的資料。

        from possible default events within the 12 months after the              期後十二個月內可能發生之違約事件而導
        reporting date; and                                                      致之虧損；及

       Write-off policy                                                          撇銷政策
       The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written off (either     倘金融資產並無實際收回前景，則其賬面總值
       partially or in full) to the extent that there is no realistic prospect   （部分或全部）將會被撇銷。該情況通常出現在本
       of recovery. This is generally the case when the Group determines         集團認為債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生足夠
       that the debtor does not have assets or sources of income that could      現金流量以償還將予撇銷之金額。
       generate sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject to the
       write-off.

       decreased:

               and investment properties;                                               資物業；

               financial position.

 1      Significant accounting policies (Continued)                          1      主要會計政策（續）

 (k)    Credit loss and impairment of assets (Continued)                     (k)    信貸虧損及資產減值（續）
 (ii)   Impairment of other assets (Continued)                               (ii)   其他資產之減值（續）
 –      Calculation of recoverable amount                                    –      計算可收回金額

        The recoverable amount of an asset is the greater of its fair               資產之可收回金額為其公允值減去出售成
        value less costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing value           本及使用價值兩者中之較高者。於評估使
        in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to                   用價值時，會使用除稅前折現率將估計未
        their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects             來現金流量折現至其現值。該折現率反映
        current market assessments of time value of money and the                   市場當時所評估之貨幣時間價值和該資產
        risks specific to the asset. Where an asset does not generate               之獨有風險。倘個別資產所產生之現金流
        cash inflows largely independent of those from other assets,                入基本上不能獨立於其他資產所產生之現
        the recoverable amount is determined for the smallest group                 金流入，則就獨立產生現金流入之最小資
        of assets that generates cash inflows independently (i.e. a                 產組合（即現金產生單位）釐定可收回金
        cash-generating unit).                                                      額。

 –      Recognition of impairment losses                                     –      確認減值虧損

        An impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss if the carrying          資產或其所屬之現金產生單位之賬面值高
        amount of an asset, or the cash-generating unit to which it                 於其可收回金額時，則會於損益確認減值
        belongs, exceeds its recoverable amount. Impairment losses                  虧損。就現金產生單位確認之減值虧損會
        recognised in respect of cash-generating units are allocated                首先分配予削減已分配至該現金產生單位
        first to reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill allocated               （或一組單位）之任何商譽之賬面值，然後
        to the cash-generating unit (or group of units) and then, to                按比例削減該單位（或一組單位）內其他資
        reduce the carrying amount of the other assets in the unit (or              產之賬面值，惟資產賬面值不可下調至低
        group of units) on a pro rata basis, except that the carrying               於其個別公允值減去出售成本（如能計量）
        value of an asset will not be reduced below its individual fair             或使用價值（如能釐定）。
        value less costs of disposal (if measurable), or value in use (if
        determinable).

 –      Reversals of impairment losses                                       –      撥回減值虧損

        In respect of assets other than goodwill, an impairment loss is             就商譽以外之資產而言，倘用以釐定可收
        reversed if there has been a favourable change in the estimates             回金額之估計發生有利變動，則會將減值
        used to determine the recoverable amount. An impairment                     虧損撥回。商譽之減值虧損不可撥回。
        loss in respect of goodwill is not reversed.

        A reversal of an impairment loss is limited to the asset’s                  所撥回之減值虧損僅限於倘若並無於過往
        carrying amount that would have been determined had no                      年度確認減值虧損而可釐定之資產賬面
        impairment loss been recognised in prior years. Reversals of                值。所撥回之減值虧損在確認撥回之年度
        impairment losses are credited to profit or loss in the year in             內計入損益。
        which the reversals are recognised.

       (l)     Inventories                                                           (l)     存貨
       Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable value.        存貨按成本值及可變現淨值兩者中之較低者入
                                                                                     賬。

       Cost is calculated using the weighted average method and comprises            成本值乃以加權平均方法計算，並包括所有購貨
       all costs of purchase, costs of conversion and other costs incurred in        成本、加工成本及將存貨運往其現時地點及達至
       bringing the inventories to their present location and condition.             現有狀態之其他成本。

       Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary           可變現淨值指正常業務過程中之估計售價減去完
       course of business less the estimated costs of completion and the             成交易之估計成本及進行銷售所需之估計成本。
       estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

       When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of those inventories           出售存貨時，其賬面值於有關收入確認期內確認
       is recognised as an expense in the period in which the related                為開支。任何存貨金額撇減至可變現淨值及存貨
       revenue is recognised. The amount of any write-down of inventories            之所有虧損均於撇減或虧損之發生期內確認為開
       to net realisable value and all losses of inventories are recognised as       支。倘存貨之撇減出現任何撥回，則於撥回出現
       an expense in the period the write-down or loss occurs. The amount            期內將費用作減額確認。
       of any reversal of any write-down of inventories is recognised as a
       reduction in the amount of inventories recognised as an expense in
       the period in which the reversal occurs.


       (m) Receivables                                                               (m) 應收款
       A receivable is recognised when the Group has an unconditional                本集團於具有無條件權利收取代價時確認應收
       right to receive consideration. A right to receive consideration is           款。倘代價到期付款前僅需待時間推移，則收取
       unconditional if only the passage of time is required before payment          代價之權利為無條件。
       of that consideration is due.

       Receivables are stated at amortised cost using the effective interest         應收款以實際利率法按攤銷成本減去信貸虧損撥
       method less allowance for credit losses (see note 1(k)(i)).                   備後入賬（見附註 1(k)(i)）。

 but not control over the underlying software asset.

 (i)    Segment results, assets and liabilities                             (i)   分部業績、資產及負債
 For the purposes of assessing segment performance and allocating           就評估分部表現及各分部間之資源配置而言，本
 resources between segments, the Group’s most senior executive              集團最高層行政管理人員根據下列基準監控各須
 management monitors the results, assets and liabilities attributable       報告分部之業績、資產及負債：
 to each reportable segment on the following bases:

 Segment assets include all tangible assets, intangible assets and          分部資產包括全部有形資產、無形資產及流動資
 current assets with the exception of interest in joint venture, deferred   產，惟於合營公司之權益、遞延稅項資產、應收
 tax assets, current tax recoverable, goodwill and unallocated              現期稅項、商譽及未分配之總公司及企業資產除
 head office and corporate assets. Segment liabilities include trade        外。分部負債包括個別分部之生產及銷售活動之
 creditors and bills payable attributable to the manufacture and sales      應付賬款及應付票據、銀行貸款及由分部直接管
 activities of the individual segments, bank loans, lease liabilities and   理之租賃負債及其他負債，惟僱員退休福利負
 other liabilities managed directly by the segments with the exception      債、遞延稅項負債、應付現期稅項及未分配之總
 of employee retirement benefit liabilities, deferred tax liabilities,      公司及企業負債除外。
 current tax payable and unallocated head office and corporate
 liabilities.

 The measure used for reporting segment profit/(loss) is “profit/           用於報告分部溢利╱（虧損）之表示方法為「經
 (loss) from operations”. To arrive at “profit/(loss) from operations”,     營溢利╱（虧損）」。為了得出「經營溢利╱（虧
 the Group’s profit/(loss) is further adjusted for items not specifically   損）」， 本 集 團 之 溢 利 ╱（ 虧 損）就 並 無 明 確 歸
 attributed to individual segments, such as finance costs, share of         於個別分部之項目（如融資成本、所佔合營公
 losses of joint venture, impairment loss on goodwill and brand name,       司虧損、商譽及品牌名稱之減值虧損、Vitasoy
 acquisition related expenses on the remaining 49% equity interest          Australia Products Pty. Ltd.（「VAP」）餘 下 49%
 in Vitasoy Australia Products Pty. Ltd. (“VAP”) and unallocated            股權的收購相關開支以及未分配之總公司及企業
 head office and corporate expenses. Income tax is not allocated to         費用）作出進一步調整。所得稅並無列入報告分
 reporting segments. Inter-segment sales are priced at cost plus a          部。分部間銷售乃按成本加邊際利潤定價。
 profit margin.

       (a)    Impairment                                                            (a)   減值
       The carrying amount of interest in joint venture was fully impaired by       截至二零二一年三月三十一日止年度，於計及
       recognising an impairment charge of $42,800,000 after considering            2019冠狀病毒病對未來銷售預測之不利影響後，
       the adverse impact of COVID-19 on future sales projections in the            通過確認減值費用 42,800,000 元，合營公司之權
       year ended 31st March 2021.                                                  益之賬面值已全數減值。

       During the year ended 31st March 2023, the performance of the                截至二零二三年三月三十一日止年度，合營公司
       joint venture recovered, leading to an improvement in level of losses        業績回升，菲律賓政府放寬檢疫限制後虧損水平
       incurred following the relaxation of quarantine restrictions by the          有所改善。儘管如此，考慮到當前累計虧損以及
       Philippines government. Nevertheless, considering the current                計算可收回金額時評估市場因素的不確定性，管
       accumulated losses and uncertainty in evaluating market factors in           理層認為毋須作出減值撥回。
       the calculation of the recoverable amount, management concluded
       that no reversal of impairment was required.


       (b)    Unrecognised share of losses                                          (b)   未確認所佔虧損

       recognised a share of loss of $22,253,000 after the capital injection.       資後已確認應佔虧損 22,253,000 元。

       As at 31st March 2023, the Group has discontinued recognising its            於二零二三年三月三十一日，本集團已終止確認
       share of losses of joint venture because its cumulative share of losses      其所佔合營公司虧損，因為其累計所佔合營公司
       in the joint venture has exceeded the aggregate of the investment            虧損已超過投資成本與股東貸款扣除其後累計減
       cost and shareholders’ loan less subsequent accumulated impairment           值虧損的總額。在本集團所佔合營公司任何未來
       losses. The Group will not resume recognition of its share of any            溢利等於過往年度未確認之累計所佔虧損前，本

       Under the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance, the Group is obliged            根據香港《僱傭條例》
                                                                                          ，本集團須在若干情況下終
       to make lump sum payments on cessation of employment in certain           止僱用某些為本集團服務至少五年之僱員時向彼
       circumstances to certain employees who have completed at least five       等支付一筆過款項。所須支付之金額視乎僱員最
       years of service with the Group. The amount payable is dependent          後所領薪金及服務年資而定，並扣減本集團退休
       on the employees’ final salary and years of service, and is reduced by    計劃下歸屬於本集團所作供款之應計權益。本集
       entitlements accrued under the Group’s retirement plans that are          團並未預留任何資產以就履行任何剩餘責任提供
       attributable to contributions made by the Group. The Group does not       所需之資金。
       set aside any assets to fund any remaining obligations.

       The Government of the Hong Kong SAR gazetted the Employment                香港特別行政區政府於二零二二年六月刊憲
       and Retirement Schemes Legislation (Offsetting Arrangement)               《2022 年僱傭及退休計劃法例（抵銷安排）
                                                                                                      （修訂）
       (Amendment) Ordinance 2022 (the “Amendment Ordinance”) in                  條例》（「該修訂條例」），取消使用僱主根據強積
       June 2022, which abolishes the use of the accrued benefits derived         金計劃作出強制性供款而產生之應計權益，以抵
       from employers’ mandatory contributions under the MPF Scheme               銷遣散費及長期服務金（「對沖安排」）。
       to offset severance payment and long service payment (“LSP”) (the
       “offsetting arrangement”).

       The Amendment Ordinance has prospective effect from a date                該修訂條例自政府公佈當日起生效。具體而言，
       to be announced by the Government. In particular, (i) employers           (i) 僱主可繼續用其強積金供款（不論在轉制日期
       may continue to use the accrued benefits derived from their MPF           之前、當日或之後所作之供款，亦不論強制性或
       contributions (irrespective of the contributions made before, on or       自願性供款）所產生之應計權益，以抵銷僱員轉
       after the transition date, and irrespective of mandatory or voluntary     制前部分之長期服務金；(ii) 應付僱員之長期服
       contributions) to offset employees’ pre-transition portion of LSP; (ii)   務金轉制前部分將按僱員緊接轉制日期前最後一
       the pre-transition portion of LSP due to employees will be calculated     個月薪金及轉制日之任職年期計算。目前，本集
       on the basis of the employee’s final month’s salary immediately           團並無任何獨立融資安排以履行其長期服務金承
       preceding the transition date and the years of service as at the          擔。預計政府將於取消削減措施後推出一項補貼
       transition date. Currently, the Group does not have any separate          計劃為僱主提供援助。
       funding arrangement in place to meet its long service payment
       obligation. The Government is expected to introduce a subsidy
       scheme to assist employers after the abolition of the reduction.

       As the amount of offsettable accrued benefits under the MPF               如取消對沖安排生效，強積金計劃下之可抵銷應
       Scheme is expected to decrease once the abolition of the offsetting       計福利金額預期將會減少，因此未來相關負面福
       arrangement takes effect, the expected reduction in the related           利預期減少會歸因於各僱員過往及目前任職年
       negative benefits in the future has been attributed to each employee’s    期，從而導致在該修訂條例頒佈當年調整過往
       past and current service periods. This leads to an adjustment for the     服務成本 15,530,000 元，並確認當期服務成本
       past service cost of $15,530,000 and a recognition of the current         728,000 元。
       service cost of $728,000 in the year of enactment of the Amendment
       Ordinance.

 (g)    Capital management                                                  (g)   資本管理
 The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to                本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團能
 safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, so that      持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格訂於
 it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for       與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，使其
 other stakeholders, by pricing products and services commensurately        能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關人士提
 with the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a reasonable   供利益。
 cost.

 The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital           本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
 structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder             以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄厚
 returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings and        資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並因應
 the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital position,          經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
 and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in
 economic conditions.

 Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital         本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比率
 structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For this       作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本集團
 purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bills payable, bank        將負債界定為應付票據、銀行貸款及租賃負債之
 loans and lease liabilities, and adjusted capital as all components of     總額，經調整資本為權益之所有組成部份減非累
 equity less unaccrued proposed dividends.                                  計擬派股息。

 The Group’s strategy, which was unchanged from the year ended              本集團之策略與截至二零二二年三月三十一日止
 31st March 2022, was to maintain the debt-to-adjusted capital              年度保持不變，將負債對經調整資本比率維持於
 ratio below 30%. In order to maintain or adjust the ratio, the Group       30% 以下。為保持或調整有關比率，本集團或會
 may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue new         調整向股東派付之股息金額、發行新股份、向股
 shares, return capital to shareholders, raise new debt financing or sell   東退回資本、籌集新債項融資或出售資產以減低
 assets to reduce debt.                                                     負債。

 Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks arises in   本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資金、
 the normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s exposures to         利率及貨幣風險。本集團面臨之有關風險及本集

 (a)    Credit risk                                                          (a)   信貸風險
 Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its      信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集團
 contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group.         財務虧損之風險。

 The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other        本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應收
 receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and exposure to        款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關信貸
 credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.                               風險持續受到監控。

 The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial                本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸評
 institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management consider         級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信貸風
 the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                                 險為低。

 The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose the            本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險之
 Group to credit risk.                                                       擔保。

 current ability to pay, and take into account information specific to       以及有關客戶營運所在之經濟環境。應收賬款及
 the customer as well as pertaining to the economic environment in           應收票據一般於發單日起計一至三個月內到期。
 which the customer operates. Trade debtors and bills receivables            一般而言，本集團不會從客戶取得抵押品。
 are generally due within one to three months from the date of billing.
 Normally, the Group does not obtain collateral from customers.
